Existing natural language methods can identify or generate candidates but often do not expose how the selected candidate completes the inference, while logic-based approaches usually assume that the required formulae and background knowledge are available. We extend a prior neuro-symbolic pipeline from missing-premise to missing-claim selection and replace binary entailment outcomes with logical-resistance scores. Top-Link uses weighted Partial MaxSAT under a single configuration of highest-confidence semantic links. We then introduce Possible-World Atom-Link Formalization (PWAL), which keeps translated formulae fixed and marginalizes logical resistance over alternative cross-formula semantic-link configurations. We evaluate PWAL on five tasks: ARCT and a CDED-derived task for missing-premise selection, iDebate- and AAE2-derived tasks for missing-claim selection, and alphaNLI for abductive hypothesis selection. Relative to Top-Link, PWAL raises strict accuracy by 2.95-30.86 percentage points and reduces tie rates by 4.57-58.00 percentage points on all five tasks. When ties receive half credit, accuracy still increases by 0.45-6.04 percentage points. PWAL also records the translated formulae, sampled link configurations, and resistance components for every comparison, providing a transparent trace of each score.

</details>


### [134] [Verifiable abstention makes AI leak diagnosis accountable in water distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18836)
*Tianwei Mu,Yue Wang,Mingzhe Yuan,Manhong Huang,Wenhong Wang,Xuerui Yin,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Hui Yang,Jun Li,Dan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将漏损定位重构为"可验证弃权下的决策问题"，通过物理驱动执行体与独立监督体（含LLM审计）的框架，在无法确证时主动弃权而非盲目猜测，将强制基线32%提升至96%的决策精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水务公司尽管AI定位器精度已达到较高水平，仍不信任其派工开挖，因为没有任何方法能证明"何时不应行动"。问题的核心是问责性（accountability）而非精度（accuracy）——现有方法无法证明其不行动的时刻，导致误开挖成本无人承担。

Method: 将漏损定位重构为在可验证弃权下的决策问题。具体架构包括：(1) 物理驱动的执行体代理，在数字孪生上对假设（泄漏、需求、传感器、阀门）进行证伪；(2) 独立的监督体代理，配合大语言模型（LLM）审计员，依据代码可验证的合约核查证据，然后认证派工、请求证据或选择弃权。

Result: 在实场级噪声下，32%的强制基线提升为96%的决策精度（仅在acted事件上）。在独立生成的基准上，仅对33处泄漏中的4处行动且全部正确。基于194个已审计真实漏点的注册数据（含孪生模拟的压力与流量），产生5次开挖派工，其中3次正确，并在全区精度下实现44%的勘察回收率。

Conclusion: 可问责的弃权机制为自主水务基础设施运营提供了一条有说服力的路径——通过"宁可少动、动则必准"的策略，用可验证的弃权换取系统的可信度与可辩护性。

Abstract: Utilities lose a substantial share of treated water to leakage, yet rarely trust artificial-intelligence localizers to dispatch crews: guessing everywhere cannot justify excavation. The gap is accountability, not accuracy: no method proves when it should not act. Here we recast leak localization as decision-making under verifiable abstention. A physics-grounded executor agent falsifies hypotheses (leak, demand, sensor, valve) against a digital twin; an independent supervisor agent, with a large-language-model (LLM) auditor, checks evidence against a code-verifiable contract, then certifies a dispatch, requests evidence or abstains. Under field-grade noise, a 32% forced baseline becomes 96% decision precision on acted events. On an independently generated benchmark it acts on only 4 of 33 leaks, all correct. A 194-event register of audited real leak locations with twin-simulated pressures and flows yields five excavation dispatches, three correct, and 44% survey recovery at full district precision. Accountable abstention offers a defensible route to autonomous water-infrastructure operation.

</details>


### [135] [ORBITER: Conflict-Aware Decision-Making for Agentic Last-Mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18846)
*Mingzhao Li,Chenxi Liu,Yan Zhao,Hao Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ORBITER是一个基于LLM的智能体系统，用于最后一公里配送中的下一订单决策。它通过决策点建模快递员服务，利用结构化报告识别固定排名器间的分歧，让LLM通过任务工具收集证据并经由独立评审者验证决策，在四个城市数据上平均优于现有SOTA基线最高9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法虽能建模订单间的时空依赖并预测快递员服务序列，但缺乏对单次下一订单决策的可解释性。将配送状态用语言描述可使LLM明确推理个体决策背后的时空和行为线索，但LLM作为直接预测器对任务呈现方式敏感且决策不可靠。因此需要一种结合LLM推理能力与可靠决策机制的框架。

Method: ORBITER将快递员服务建模为一系列决策点，每个决策点包含快递员的时空状态和可见订单，并暴露局部权衡以供建模和验证。系统包含：(1) 固定排序器（proposers）对候选订单进行排名；(2) 结构化报告识别各排序器排名分歧之处；(3) LLM使用任务专用工具收集关于领先候选方案的证据；(4) 独立评审者（critic）基于证据检查最终决策的正确性。

Result: 在四个城市的数据上进行广泛评估，ORBITER平均优于现有最先进的基线方法最高达9.2%，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ORBITER通过将决策点建模、多排序器分歧识别、LLM证据收集与独立评审验证相结合的智能体框架，有效提升了最后一公里配送中下一订单决策的准确性和可解释性，显著超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Last-mile delivery aims to handle dynamically arriving orders with couriers while modeling complex spatial and temporal correlations. Recent learning-based methods model spatiotemporal dependencies among orders to predict courier service sequences, but leave next-order decision making unexplained. Describing the current delivery state in language allows LLMs to reason explicitly about the spatial, temporal, and behavioral cues behind an individual decision. As direct predictors, however, LLMs remain sensitive to task presentation and often produce unreliable decisions. To address these challenges, we introduce ORBITER, an agentic Order Arbiter for next-order decision-making in last-mile delivery. ORBITER models courier service through decision points, each containing the courier's spatiotemporal state and visible orders and exposing local trade-offs for modeling and verification. Fixed proposers rank the candidates, and a structured report identifies where their rankings disagree. The LLM uses task-specific tools to gather evidence on the leading alternatives, while an independent critic checks the resulting decision against that evidence. We conduct extensive evaluations on data in four cities, where ORBITER outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines by up to 9.2% on average showing its effectiveness.

</details>


### [136] [SkillGate: Training In-Policy Skill Selection in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18852)
*Qingyao Li,Wenxiang Jiao,Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"选择器信用饥饿"（selector credit starvation）问题，指出在智能体框架中，奖励信号几乎无法训练"读取哪个技能"的决策。作者提出SkillGate方法，通过将token支持划分为两个不相交的信用通道，使结果信用只到达执行token，而独立的动作局部优势恰好到达技能命名token。在五个智能体基准和16个候选技能的设置下，SkillGate将9B策略的试验成功率从40.8%提升至53.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架将程序性知识打包为技能文件，策略需在回合中途自行决定读取哪个技能，但现有训练信号无法对此进行训练。默认方案——对候选列表施加结果奖励RL——存在结构性缺陷：广播式序列级优势下，命名所选技能的少量token承担极小的损失份额，且随轨迹变长，其继承的信用错误符号越发严重。

Method: 首先识别并命名"选择器信用饥饿"现象，通过审计完整运行轨迹的训练伪影实证验证其三个属性随视野长度单调恶化。随后提出SkillGate：将token支持划分为两个不相交的信用通道——结果信用仅到达执行token，独立的动作局部优势恰好到达技能命名token，且仅在轨迹中唯一一次读取为正确时取正值。

Result: 在五个智能体基准、16个候选技能的设置下，SkillGate将9B策略的试验成功率从40.8%提升至53.2%，显著优于将相同预算用于纯结果奖励的效果；同时将暴露于误导候选技能的概率降低三分之二，并减少读取技能的数量。

Conclusion: SkillGate通过构造性设计消除了选择器信用饥饿问题，证明技能选择决策可以通过独立的动作局部信用通道得到有效训练，优于传统结果奖励RL，同时提升效率与安全性（减少误导候选的暴露）。

Abstract: Agent frameworks increasingly package procedural knowledge as skills: instruction files an agent reads on demand, while public libraries now hold thousands of them. Which skill to read has thus become a decision the policy itself makes in the middle of an episode, yet no existing signal trains it. We show that the default remedy, outcome-rewarded RL over the candidate slate, cannot teach it, for a structural reason we identify and name selector credit starvation: under a broadcast, sequence-level advantage, the few tokens that name the chosen skill carry a vanishing share of the loss, and the credit they inherit is increasingly wrong-signed as trajectories lengthen. A correct choice is punished whenever the execution after it fails, even though the choice itself is among the most valuable decisions in the trajectory. Auditing a completed run's own training artifacts confirms all three properties, each worsening monotonically with horizon. SkillGate removes the failure by construction: it partitions the token support into two disjoint credit channels, outcome credit reaching only execution tokens, and a separate action-local advantage reaching exactly the skill-naming tokens, positive only when a trajectory's single read is the correct one. On five agentic benchmarks under a 16-candidate slate, SkillGate lifts a 9B policy from 40.8% to 53.2% trial success, well ahead of the identical budget spent on outcome reward alone, while cutting exposure to misleading candidates by two thirds and reading fewer skills.

</details>


### [137] [DentAgent: Evidence-Centric Multi-Agent Coordination for Multimodal Dental Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18878)
*Zijie Meng,Xiwei Dai,Yixuan Tang,Jin Hao,Yang Feng,Fudong Zhu,Xiaoqiang Liu,Shaosheng Cao,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DentAgent，一个以证据为中心的多智能体框架，通过编排器协调五个跨模态专业智能体，将领域工具转换的结构化证据记录存储在共享证据黑板中，实现多模态牙科推理的可追踪化。在四个基准测试中取得领先性能，甚至在多标签诊断上超越高级专家17.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔疾病影响全球数十亿人，需要整合领域知识、放射影像、口内照片和3D牙科数据等多源异构证据的准确可靠牙科评估。然而现有牙科AI系统多为模态或任务特化，虽然VLM支持灵活的牙科问答，但直接生成的回答使证据隐式且不可追踪。

Method: DentAgent采用证据中心的多智能体框架，包含一个编排器（Orchestrator）协调五个覆盖不同模态的专业智能体。每个专业智能体利用领域工具将观测结果转换为结构化证据记录，通过证据黑板（Evidence Blackboard）管理共享证据状态，跟踪覆盖度、缺口和冲突后再生成响应。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，DentAgent展现了领先性能，在多标签诊断任务上甚至超过高级专家17.3个百分点，验证了其在广泛适用的可追踪多模态牙科推理中的价值。

Conclusion: 标准化证据表示将孤立牙科能力整合为统一智能体工作流，凸显其作为人群口腔健康评估与管理技术基础的潜力。

Abstract: Oral diseases affect billions of people worldwide, underscoring a pressing need for accurate and reliable dental assessment that integrates heterogeneous evidence from domain knowledge, radiographs, intraoral photographs, and 3D dental data. Most existing dental AI systems remain modality- or task-specific. Although recent vision-language models support flexible dental question answering, directly generated response leaves evidence implicit and untraceable. To address these limitations, we introduce DentAgent, an evidence-centric multi-agent framework, in which the Orchestrator coordinate five specialized agents spanning various modalities. Each specialist utilizes domain tools to convert observations into structured evidence records. The Evidence Blackboard manages these records as a shared evidence state, tracking coverage, gaps, and conflicts before response generation. This standardized evidence representation integrates isolated dental capabilities into a unified agentic workflow. Across four benchmarks, DentAgent demonstrates leading performance, even surpassing the senior specialists by 17.3 percentage points on multi-label diagnosis, which supports its value for broadly applicable and traceable multimodal dental reasoning, and highlights its potential as a technical foundation for population oral health assessment and management.

</details>


### [138] [Training-Free Inference-Time Self-Reflection and Cost-Bounded Early Stopping for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18884)
*Wei Yu,Suxing Liu,Minjie Yu,Jiahao Wang,Zhijian Zheng,Haocheng Deng,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoResearcher，一种免训练、推理时（inference-time）的成本约束自反思协议，在单个冻结LLM骨干上迭代"生成→自批评→修订"直至最大深度D或CONFIRMED哨兵触发早停。零梯度更新下嵌入四个自反思元奖励组件，在BBH、GSM8K、MATH及Qwen2.5-72B上跨域验证，CONFIRMED早停以约2.1次生成/题终止82-88%的条目且准确率持平；纯推理基准上未超过95% Wilson区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练推理LLM（如GRPO）成本高昂且需要可控环境，导致每项贡献都须投入完整训练流程。现有方法缺乏一种无需梯度更新的推理时成本约束自反思方案。

Method: 提出EvoResearcher协议：在单个冻结LLM骨干上迭代"生成→自批评→修订"，直到达到最大深度D或收到CONFIRMED哨兵（隐式早停，让骨干在严格计算预算下自验证答案）。四个元奖励组件（正确性、效率、反思深度、工具调用多样性）作为设计原则以提示级别机制实例化，零梯度更新即可获得收益。在BBH（100题）、GSM8K（500）、MATH（500）及Qwen2.5-72B上跨模型复现验证。

Result: 在纯BBH上协议未将准确率提升至超过95% Wilson区间；其核心价值在于成本约束的自验证——CONFIRMED早停以相同准确率终止82-88%的条目，平均每问题约2.1次生成。工具调用多样性以提示级形式验证，环境级与多智能体扩展留作未来工作蓝图。

Conclusion: EvoResearcher的价值不在于提升干净基准的准确率上限，而在于以极低计算成本实现可信的自验证与早停。实验限定于纯推理基准，工具调用多样性仅在提示级验证，环境级与多智能体扩展是未来方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning training of reasoning LLMs (e.g., GRPO) is expensive and requires a controllable environment, committing every contribution to a full training pipeline. We present EvoResearcher, a training-free, inference-time protocol that adds cost-bounded self-reflection to a single frozen LLM backbone. The protocol iterates generate -> self-critique -> revise until a maximum depth D is reached or the critique returns the CONFIRMED sentinel, an implicit early stop that lets the backbone self-verify its answer under a strict compute budget. Four self-reflective meta-reward components (correctness, efficiency, reflection depth, tool-call diversity) act as design principles instantiated as prompt-level mechanisms, so their benefits accrue with zero gradient updates. We validate the protocol on Big-Bench Hard (100 questions) and establish cross-domain behavior on GSM8K (500) and MATH (500) on the same frozen backbone, with cross-model replication on Qwen2.5-72B. All experiments use pure-reasoning benchmarks; the tool-call diversity component is validated in prompt-level form, and the environment-level and multi-agent extensions are design blueprints left to future work. On clean BBH the protocol does not raise accuracy beyond the 95% Wilson interval; its value is cost-bounded self-verification, with the CONFIRMED early stop terminating 82-88% of items at equal accuracy (about 2.1 generations per question).

</details>


### [139] [Syntactic Simplification of OWL Class Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18899)
*Alkid Baci,N'Dah Jean Kouagou,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES（Class Expression Simplifier）算法，用于描述逻辑中类表达式的语法简化，通过系统地应用重写规则消除冗余并识别更简单的等价表达式，在保持语义等价的前提下减少表示复杂度，提升推理效率和可读性。算法已集成到开源Python框架OWLAPY中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类表达式学习往往产生复杂且难以解释的OWL类表达式，这些表达式不仅难以理解，还增加了推理负担。然而，通过理论上合理的简化原则，可以在不改变形式语义的前提下降低这种复杂度。作者希望通过语法简化来提升类表达式的可读性和推理效率。

Method: 提出CES算法，这是一种针对描述逻辑（DL）中类表达式的语法简化算法。CES在保持形式语义的前提下，系统地应用重写规则来消除冗余并发现更简单但等价的表达式，从而在不改变逻辑蕴含关系的情况下产生更紧凑和易于阅读的表示形式。

Result: 在两个中等规模本体上学习了类表达式进行有效性评估，实验结果表明在推理效率上取得了可测量的改进，同时减少了表达式的冗长程度（verbosity）。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于使基于本体的应用程序更易于访问、维护和扩展，对知识图谱构建、语义搜索和Web规模推理具有直接影响。CES已在开源Python框架OWLAPY中实现并公开可用。

Abstract: Class expression learning often produces complex OWL class expressions that are difficult to interpret and reason over. However, by following theoretically grounded simplification principles, this complexity can be reduced. In this paper, we propose Class Expression Simplifier (CES), a novel algorithm for the syntactic simplification of class expressions in Description Logics (DL). CES aims to preserve formal semantics while reducing representational complexity. It systematically applies rewriting rules to eliminate redundancies and identify simpler yet equivalent expressions, thereby producing more compact and human-readable representations without altering logical entailments. We evaluate the effectiveness of CES on class expressions learned from two medium-sized ontologies, demonstrating measurable improvements in reasoning efficiency and reductions in verbosity. This work contributes to the broader goal of making ontology-driven applications more accessible, maintainable, and scalable, with direct implications for knowledge graph construction, semantic search, and Web-scale reasoning. CES is implemented within the open-source Python framework OWLAPY and is publicly available.

</details>


### [140] [\textsc{TestifAI}: Tomography-Based Testing for Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18900)
*Arooj Arif,Tobias Hartung,Elena Botoeva,Alexandros Koliousis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TestifAI，一个深度学习测试框架，通过部分模型断层扫描（partial model tomography）技术，从低阶（1-2个）扰动测试结果中重建高维扰动空间中的模型行为，高效且准确地估计组合扰动的鲁棒性，减少了60-80%的推理次数，聚合鲁棒性估计误差低于7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习测试框架缺乏系统性地探索和概括组合扰动空间中鲁棒性的能力。随着AI系统在安全关键领域（如自动驾驶）的部署增加，对模型鲁棒性进行全面测试的需求日益迫切，但穷举所有扰动组合会导致指数级数量的测试，成本过高。

Method: TestifAI允许用户将操作条件指定为语义输入扰动（如模糊、亮度、缩放）和离散严重级别（低、中、高）的结构化空间，并查询任意组合下的模型鲁棒性。核心创新是部分模型断层扫描（partial model tomography），即仅通过在低阶（1-2个）扰动投影上执行的测试来重建多扰动空间中的模型行为，并训练辅助模型预测高阶（3-4个）扰动测试结果，避免执行指数数量的测试。

Result: 在五个图像和语言分类任务上的实验表明，TestifAI能够从低阶（1和2个扰动）观察中预测高阶（3和4个扰动）测试结果，聚合鲁棒性估计误差低于7%，同时将推理次数减少了60-80%。

Conclusion: TestifAI证明了通过低阶扰动测试来高效准确地估计高阶组合扰动鲁棒性的可行性，为深度学习模型的鲁棒性测试提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，尤其适用于安全关键的应用场景。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical application domains (e.g., autonomous driving), associated risks increase too. Deep learning models underlying modern AI systems, therefore, must undergo thorough testing to ensure their correct behaviour. A single robustness test involves thousands of inferences to empirically verify if a model's outputs remain stable under a bounded perturbation of its inputs. However, existing testing frameworks lack the means to systematically explore and summarise robustness across a combinatorial space of perturbations.
  We propose TestifAI, a deep learning testing framework for efficient and accurate estimation of robustness against combinations of perturbations. TestifAI enables users to specify operational conditions as structured spaces of semantic input perturbations (e.g., image blur, brightness and zoom) and discrete severity levels (e.g., low, medium and high). Users can query model robustness for any combination (e.g., "low blur, high brightness, and medium zoom"). To achieve efficiency and accuracy, TestifAI introduces partial model tomography, a novel approach to reconstructing model behaviour in a multi-perturbation space from tests that apply only a small number of perturbations (lower-order projections). To estimate robustness against at least three perturbations, TestifAI trains an auxiliary model on the results of tests involving up to two perturbations only, avoiding execution of an exponential number of tests. Our experiments on five image and language classification tasks show that TestifAI can predict higher-order (3 and 4 perturbations) test outcomes from low-order (1 and 2 perturbations) observations with an aggregate robustness estimation error of less than 7%, while reducing the number of inferences by 60-80%.

</details>


### [141] [Breaking the weakest link to evade vision language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18938)
*Ilan Zini,Boussad Addad,Katarzyna Kapusta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在视觉输入遭受对抗性扰动时的脆弱性，提出了一种仅针对视觉编码器进行梯度优化的高效攻击方法，并在多个开源VLM上证明了微小的、人眼不可察觉的扰动能够显著改变模型的文本输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在现实和安全性关键的多模态AI系统中被广泛部署，但其对抗性鲁棒性，特别是针对多模态对齐的逃逸攻击，仍未被充分探索。需要系统性地研究VLM对对抗性扰动的脆弱性，以推动更安全的AI系统开发。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的攻击方法，该方法仅对VLM的视觉编码器进行优化而非对整个多模态架构进行优化，从而显著降低攻击的计算成本和资源需求。研究了两种攻击设置：非目标攻击（破坏模型对原始图像的解释）和目标攻击（强制模型生成与原始图像无关的特定语义描述）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL、Granite-Vision、FastVLM和Phi-3.5-Vision等多个开源VLM上进行了评估，结果表明微小且人眼不可察觉的扰动能够大幅改变模型生成的文本解释，验证了该攻击方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了现代VLM对对抗性操纵的脆弱性，强调需要改进多模态AI系统中的鲁棒性和安全机制。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a critical component of multimodal AI systems, enabling joint reasoning over visual and textual inputs in real-world and safety-critical applications. Despite their growing deployment, the robustness of VLMs against adversarial threats remains insufficiently explored, particularly in the context of evasion attacks targeting multimodal alignment. In this work, we investigate the vulnerability of VLMs to adversarial perturbations applied to visual inputs and study two attack settings: untargeted attacks, where the goal is to disrupt the model's interpretation of the original image, and targeted attacks, where the adversary aims to force the model to generate a specific semantic description unrelated to the original image. To efficiently generate adversarial examples, we propose a gradient-based attack method that performs optimization exclusively on the vision encoder of the VLM rather than on the entire multimodal architecture. This design significantly reduces the computational cost and resource requirements of the attack while maintaining strong effectiveness. We evaluate our approach on several open-source VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Granite-Vision, FastVLM, and Phi-3.5-Vision, and show that small, human-imperceptible perturbations can substantially alter the textual interpretation produced by the models. Our findings highlight the vulnerability of modern VLMs to adversarial manipulation and emphasize the need for improved robustness and security mechanisms in multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [142] [A Theory of Post-hoc Debate Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19002)
*Xiang Yin,Adam Dejl,Antonio Rago,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于辩论评判的正式理论框架，定义了可复现性、鲁棒性、根基性和可解释性等性质，并在事实验证场景中比较了基于LLM的评判与基于计算论证语义的评判，发现二者准确率相近，但后者提供更强的形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论已成为增强型AI（尤其LLM代理）提升性能、可解释性和用户参与度的重要方法，但现有辩论的最终评判通常由外部LLM事后决定，缺乏系统性理论指导。研究者希望填补这一空白，提出对辩论评判的正式理论要求，并比较不同评判方法的优劣。

Method: 首先识别辩论评判在一般场合下应满足的正式性质（可复现性、鲁棒性、根基性和可解释性）；然后在事实验证场景中，从形式化和实验两个层面检验两种评判方法——基于LLM作为评判者的变体方法，以及源自计算论证的正式语义方法——对这些性质的满足情况。

Result: 两种评判方法在准确率上表现相近，但基于LLM的评判方法可能缺乏计算论证语义方法所提供的正式保证。

Conclusion: 论证语义被视为辩论驱动AI中有原则评判者的理想候选对象，相较于LLM作为评判者的方法能提供更强的形式化保障。

Abstract: Debates have recently emerged as a useful methodology for agentic AI to improve performance as well as to aid explainability and user engagement. For example, LLM-empowered agents may debate internally (with themselves) and/or externally (with other agents). In many settings where debates are used, debates' outcomes and resulting outputs are determined post-hoc by external judges, often LLMs. In this paper we develop and test a novel theory of debate judgement applicable to all settings where agents engage in debates by providing pros and cons for their opinions therein. Specifically, we identify a number of formal properties that debate judgement may be required to satisfy in general, as concerns reproducibility, robustness, groundedness and explainability. Then, we explore their satisfaction formally and/or experimentally, for claim verification settings, for two specific alternative debate judgement methods: variants of the LLMs as a judge idea and formal semantics drawn from computational argumentation. We show that the two methods give similar accuracy performances but the former may lack formal guarantees that the latter brings. Overall, our study indicates argumentation semantics as an ideal candidate for principled judges in debate-driven AI.

</details>


### [143] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了前沿浏览器大语言模型（LLMs）在从研究文章中提取高情境化信息方面的能力，通过四种逐步升级的工作流揭示了模型在数据提取上的优势和局限性，最终提出了一种"专家规定证据标准、模型交叉检查、研究者解决争议"的可审计人机协作分工方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从研究文章中准确提取细化且情境化的数据是一项耗时费力的工作，传统人工方法效率低下，需要探索是否可以利用大语言模型来规模化这一过程，同时保持专家监督以确保数据质量。

Method: 论文设计了四种逐步升级的工作流进行评测：1）使用专家人工编写的提示词配合研究文章，评估各前沿LLM的数据提取能力和科学情境理解水平；2）仅提供简单指令，让LLM自动编写自身提示词，比较其与专家提示词的提取效果；3）测试LLM自主发现研究文献的能力，检验其是否会遗漏或凭空捏造参考文献；4）评估LLM根据已发布指南创建新数据集的能力，并与人类专家评判结果进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：大多数前沿LLM在给定专家提示词时表现良好，但在解读科学情境和细微差异方面存在困难；LLM自行编写的提示词效果几乎与专家编写的相当；自主文献发现是难点，智能体常遗漏参考文献或产生幻觉；LLM基于指南创建的数据集与人类专家评判结果高度接近，但仍需人类介入兜底。

Conclusion: 该研究定义了可审计的劳动力分工框架：专家设定证据标准，模型负责重复提取和交叉检查，研究人员解决存在争议的案例。这一方案为在不放弃专家监督的前提下规模化科学数据策展提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Memory and Reflection Multi-Agent System for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19029)
*Pradeep Murugesan,Luoxiao Yang,Xueli Chen,Xinqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为AMR（自适应记忆与反思）的多智能体医疗问答系统，通过专门的智能体配备记忆和反思反馈机制来检索相关既有案例并改进推理，同时利用复杂度评估将问题路由至单人、协作或升级工作流，并辅以共识模块和伦理监督模块。在MedQA和MedMCQA基准上取得了优于多个基线的性能，消融实验表明智能体专属记忆、反思和外部检索的组合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗问答系统通常基于单智能体架构和静态检索，缺乏适应性、持久记忆和结构化决策能力，难以应对需要事实知识与细微推理的复杂医疗案例。

Method: 提出多智能体AMR框架：1）专门智能体配备专用记忆和基于反思的反馈机制，用于检索相关先例案例并改进后续推理；2）复杂度评估模块将问题路由至单人、协作或升级工作流；3）共识模块支持推理整合，伦理监督模块负责输出审查。

Result: 在MedQA和MedMCQA基准上取得了优于多个基线模型的性能；消融实验证明智能体专属记忆、反思和外部检索三者结合可获得最强性能。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆与反馈机制对构建更可信的医疗智能体具有重要潜力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Accurate and responsible medical question answering (QA) is important in healthcare, where complex cases require factual knowledge and nuanced reasoning. Existing medical QA systems, typically based on single-agent architectures and static retrieval, often lack adaptability, persistent memory, and structured decision-making. This work introduces an adaptive memory and reflection (AMR) agentic system, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents use dedicated memory and reflection-based feedback to retrieve relevant prior cases and improve subsequent reasoning. Complexity assessment routes questions through solo, collaborative, or escalated workflows, while consensus and ethical overseer modules support reasoning consolidation and output review. Evaluation on MedQA and MedMCQA demonstrates strong performance compared with several baselines. Ablation studies show that combining agent-specific memory, reflection, and external retrieval yields the strongest performance. These findings highlight the potential of structured memory and feedback for developing more trustworthy medical agents. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mm-air/AMR-Agent.

</details>


### [145] [Eureka: Task-Conditioned Meta-Agent Orchestration for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19047)
*Alizer Wong,Heng Cui,Yi Tan,Xiongchao Zhan,Liang Lin,Yuxiang Guo,Zhaorong Dai,Zixin Zeng,Wenyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Eureka，一种任务条件下的元智能体架构，通过动态义务图编译长时程任务，在执行中通过滚动时域规划、架构升级和最小充分编译形成具有专门状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑的宏智能体。实验在递归任务、证书生成、上下文压缩、增量处理、并发执行等方面均取得显著成果，并在理论发现和数学猜想研究领域展示了应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学智能体能力受限于仅依赖基础模型，缺乏能够匹配任务认知结构的架构。需要一种动态架构形成机制，使智能体能根据任务需求自适应地组织专门化的内部结构与执行策略，从而在长时程、递归和并发等复杂任务中展现更优表现。

Method: Eureka采用任务条件化元智能体架构，核心机制包括：(1) 将长时程任务编译为带显式接受语义的动态义务图；(2) 通过滚动时域规划、架构升级和最小充分编译形成宏智能体，每个宏智能体配备专门的状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑；(3) 当瓶颈重复出现时，借助成本-收益门控的演化机制在约束下更新局部架构；同时建立了关于遗憾、规划失效、摊销、子树接口、可串行化和验证的理论结果。

Result: 实验结果显示：Eureka完成170/170递归任务并生成3,948个证书且无误接受；主动上下文压缩将中位数输入从9,490降至4,005个token；增量处理在12,000个任务中避免65.38%重复计算；16,000个并发执行串行化一致。同一元智能体实例化出理论发现智能体和数学/猜想智能体，前者在量子过程和时空理论中取得结构性结果，后者识别黎曼假说研究瓶颈并将Suzuki局部Weil二次型正性证书推进至0 < a <= 69/200，达到(log 2)/2的99.55%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，科学智能体的能力不仅取决于基础模型，更取决于能否形成匹配任务认知结构的架构。Eureka通过动态义务图和宏智能体机制，为长时程科学推理任务提供了可验证、可扩展、可串行化的架构范式。

Abstract: We present Eureka, a task-conditioned Meta-Agent architecture that compiles long-horizon tasks into dynamic obligation graphs with explicit acceptance semantics. During execution, Eureka forms Macro-Agents with specialized state, memory, operators, tools, verifiers, and local topology via receding-horizon planning, architecture promotion, and minimal-sufficient compilation. When bottlenecks recur, cost-benefit-gated evolution updates the local architecture under constraints. Theoretically, we establish results on regret, planning invalidation, amortization, subtree interfaces, serializability, and verification. Experimentally, Eureka completes 170/170 recursive tasks and generates 3,948 certificates with no false acceptances. Active context compresses median input from 9,490 to 4,005 tokens; incremental processing avoids 65.38% recomputation across 12,000 tasks; 16,000 concurrent executions serialize consistently. The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [146] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体进行端到端后训练的能力，区分了"执行级能力"与"策略级能力"，发现智能体在训练轨迹中过早锁定高层次的训练策略，整个剩余预算都花在局部调整上。通过大规模实验检验三种可能的解释（经验缺失、指导缺失、推理不足），最终得出结论：智能体缺少的不是经验、指导或推理计算，而是一种在执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体已经能够自主完成端到端的模型后训练（写代码、启动训练、评估检查点、改进性能），这引发了"AI训练AI"的展望。然而作者认为现有视角混淆了两种截然不同的能力——执行级能力（在选定训练策略内迭代）与策略级能力（根据实验证据修订高层判断）。这一区分对于理解智能体后训练能力的本质局限至关重要，也为改进方向提供了理论框架。

Method: 作者首先分析了一个大规模公开的后训练轨迹语料库，发现不同任务中智能体的训练策略在开始时就被锁定，剩余预算全部用于所选策略内的局部调整。随后通过三种逐级增强的干预措施检验自然解释：（1）经验驱动的脚手架——提升执行能力；（2）人类指导——重定向初始策略；（3）增加推理计算——增强推理能力。通过系统性对比这些干预的效果来定位瓶颈。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）经验驱动的脚手架显著提升了执行能力（GSM8K提升12.6分，HumanEval提升40.8分），但策略始终静态不变；（2）人类指导能有效重定向初始策略，但训练一旦开始智能体便退回到局部调整循环；（3）额外推理计算在较简单任务上有收益，但在最难任务上几乎没有提升。

Conclusion: 智能体缺乏的既不是经验、指导，也不是推理计算，而是一种在执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制。这意味着要实现真正的"AI-for-AI"，需要研究如何赋予智能体在运行中主动质疑和修正自身高层策略的能力，而非仅仅增强其执行或推理能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [147] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein熵值风险（WEVaR），用最优传输球替代相对熵球来构建风险度量，克服了熵值风险无法覆盖名义模型认为不可能但实际可达的灾难事件的问题，并给出了变分对偶、风险层级位置、鲁棒动态规划算子及安全界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵值风险（EVaR）通过相对熵球刻画模型不确定性，但其固定半径的熵球无法涵盖名义模型认为不可能（概率为零）的灾难情形，而这恰恰是安全智能体必须对冲的尾部风险。为此需要一种能触及可达灾难的风险度量。

Method: 采用最优传输（Wasserstein）球替代相对熵球，由此定义Wasserstein熵值风险（WEVaR）。推导其变分对偶形式（呈现与熵公式相似的形态，其中逆温度变为传输价格），在风险层级中定位，并证明其能覆盖熵度量忽略的可达灾难。进一步用信念熵驱动传输半径，得到闭式鲁棒动态规划算子，其保守程度随信念收敛而收缩，并给出安全sandwich界与sharp安全切换。

Result: 理论推导出WEVaR的变分对偶，数值验证了两种对偶性；证明了该度量能在风险层级中占据确定位置，并能覆盖熵度量无法触及的可达灾难；基于信念熵驱动半径得到闭式鲁棒动态规划算子，具备认证的安全性sandwich界和sharp的安全切换特性。

Conclusion: Wasserstein熵值风险为"未知时谨慎、确定时大胆"的智能体安全决策提供了更完备的理论工具，尤其弥补了熵风险在灾难对冲上的盲区，且具备可计算的对偶形式和动态规划性质。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


### [148] [Tuning the Stochastic Machine: A Systems Engineer's Operating Model for Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19125)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从系统工程视角指出，LLM助手错误纠正措施随会话结束而失效的根因是运维（operations）问题而非工具问题。作者将LLM技术栈映射到传统机器（固件、模块、配置、内存），识别出映射失效处（随机生成、概率性绑定的配置、缺乏通用退役/校验阶段），由此推导出以错误循环为核心的七项运维原则，并给出测量框架与实验室研究设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家纠正LLM助手错误后，纠正措施随会话终止而丢失，同类错误反复出现。现有工具已具备持久化纠正的机制，但缺乏治理这些机制的系统性规范，作者认为这是运维纪律缺失导致的运营问题。

Method: 作者以三十年系统工程师经验，将LLM技术栈类比映射到传统计算系统（固化硅片、固件、可加载模块、持久化配置、易失内存），系统性地定位映射失效的三个关键点，并据此推导出以错误循环为核心的七项运维原则，结合作者自身实践的三个案例（包含一个"治理措施本身变成了所防范危害"的典型失控案例）进行说明。

Result: 提出了一套七原则运维纪律，核心是错误循环机制；用三个实际案例验证了机制的有效性与失控风险（一个控制项静默演变为其本欲防范的危害）；并给出了配套的测量框架和需通过实验室研究验证的假设。

Conclusion: LLM纠错失效本质是运维治理缺失（缺乏版本溯源、复发监控、对抗性指标、过时规则退役机制），而非工具能力不足。解决需建立系统化的运维纪律，并须通过实证研究加以验证。

Abstract: When an expert corrects an LLM assistant's error, the correction usually dies with the session, and the error class returns. I argue this is an operations problem, not a tooling problem: mechanisms for persisting corrections exist and are shipping, but the discipline for governing them -- versioning with provenance, recurrence monitoring, counter-metrics, retirement of stale rules -- does not. Writing as a systems engineer of thirty years, I map the LLM stack onto the machines my profession already operates (frozen silicon, firmware, loadable modules, persistent configuration, volatile memory), identify where the mapping fails (stochastic generation, configuration that binds only probabilistically, no general-purpose retirement (verification) stage by default), and derive from the failures a seven-principle operating discipline with an error loop at its core. Three cases from my own practice illustrate the mechanism, among them a control that silently became the exact harm it was built to prevent. I close with the measurement framework this view implies and the lab study required to test it.

</details>


### [149] [Grouping the Stochastic Machine: Precision, Not Capability, as the Frontier Metric for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19140)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用"精度（precision）"而非"能力（capability）"来区分前沿语言模型：能力是平均输出落在目标上，而精度是输出围绕目标的集中度。作者论证精度是可测的、非循环的、能指导决策的指标，提出了分组度量的评测框架，并通过实际运行验证了方法及其局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿语言模型的基准测试、营销和对比都聚焦于"能力"——即最佳或平均输出所能达到的水平。但模型在准确率上已经饱和，平均输出都能命中目标。真正区分系统的维度变成"精度"——在相同重复请求下输出围绕目标的集中程度。基准文化系统性地只报告集中趋势（均值）而忽视离散度（方差），这衡量了错误的轴。

Method: 借用射击术语区分"能力"（平均弹着点位置）与"可靠性/精度"（弹着群大小）。提出测量方案：在同一固定的确定性评分任务集上，以固定温度多次运行同一模型，计算每个任务的输出一致性（per-task consistency），无需模型作为评审器，避免循环性。定义了一个分组度量（grouping metric），并搭建了评测框架（harness）。

Result: 首次实际运行（后已被复现）展示了方法的价值与局限：通过一条简单规则，一个测得的分组差距被完全弥合（0/5提升到5/5）；但由规则本身撰写的任务集却未发现任何价值，因为前沿模型已内化了这些显性的良好实践。这说明：纪律（discipline）的价值必须通过真实任务的测量来评估，而不能从自己的规则手册中构建。

Conclusion: 精度而非能力才是前沿系统之间的区分维度。该测量不仅是描述性的，还能指导决策：分离"一致失败"（紧群偏离中心，可通过第一篇文章中的操作纪律校正，即瞄准调整）与"散乱失败"（宽群，只能通过更换模型或采样方式解决，即换枪问题）。追踪人机协作系统随时间的分组情况，能为第一篇文章的实地研究提供所需的复利信号。

Abstract: Frontier language models are compared, marketed, and benchmarked on capability -- what their best or average output can achieve. I argue this measures the wrong axis. The models have saturated accuracy: their mean output lands on the target. What now separates one system from another in practice is precision: how tightly concentrated their outputs are around that target across repeated, identical requests. Borrowing the marksman's distinction, capability is where the average shot lands; reliability is the size of the group. I make three claims. First, precision, not capability, is the frontier differentiator between systems, and benchmark culture systematically fails to measure it, reporting central tendency rather than spread. Second, precision is measurable, cheaply and without circularity, by running a fixed suite of deterministically scored tasks many times at fixed temperature and computing the per-task consistency of outcomes -- no model-in-the-loop grader required. Third, the measurement is not merely descriptive but decision-guiding: it separates consistent failures (a tight group off-centre, correctable by the operating discipline of Paper 1 -- a sight adjustment) from scattered failures (a wide group, correctable only by changing the model or its sampling -- a rifle problem). I define a grouping metric, specify a harness, and show how tracking a human-AI pair's grouping over time yields the compounding signal that Paper 1's field study requires. A first real run, since replicated, illustrates both the method and its most important limit: one measured gap was closed completely by a single rule (0/5 -> 5/5), while a suite of tasks authored from the rules themselves found no value, because a frontier model already embodies explicit good practice -- establishing that a discipline's worth is found by measurement on real work, not constructed from its own rulebook.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond the Transcript: Detecting Covert Co ordination in Latent Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19161)
*Ramneet Kaur,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar,Jugad Singh,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可验证潜在对齐（VLA）框架，用于监控和引导语言模型智能体之间的私有连续隐藏状态通信通道，防止隐蔽的有害合谋。VLA通过共享事件标识符将私有潜在状态与公开行为关联，实现匹配因果分析，并设计了中性训练的三层监控器、可引导性框架及多智能体拍卖基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体可通过公共转录中不可见的连续隐藏状态进行通信，这为隐蔽的有害协调创造了机会，而现有的监控手段无法观测这些私有通道，亟需有效的监控与引导机制。

Method: VLA框架包含三个贡献：(1) 三层中性监控器，结合表征异常检测、反事实动作分布影响和稀疏自编码器解释支持；(2) 可引导性框架，覆盖黑盒行为指令和白盒匹配中性反事实两种模式；(3) 在受控多智能体拍卖基准上进行评估，涵盖同构/异构模型对、多智能体扩展性和干预有效性。监控器仅使用中性样本训练，不依赖攻击样本。

Result: 顺序监控器在同构智能体上达到平均AUROC 0.993，异构配对为0.854（文本与潜在合谋行为合并为正例）。在Qwen3-0.6B拍卖中（25-100个竞标者），监控仅需相对所有可能定向对的小部分归一化负载；全白盒引导实现100%出价分布恢复，并将合谋低出价行为降低47.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 受控研究表明，所评估的私有通道攻击可以在不预训练监控器攻击样本的情况下被监控，并在具备匹配反事实访问权限时得到有效缓解。全白盒引导的重放匹配中性反事实在构造上实现了精确恢复，作为合理性检查验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: Language-model agents can communicate through continuous hidden states that are invisible in public transcripts, creating opportunities for covert harmful coordination. We introduce Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA), an activation-aware framework for monitoring and steering these private communication channels. For every monitored decision, VLA links the private latent-state record and channel status to the resulting public action using a shared event identifier, enabling matched causal analysis. Our first contribution is a neutral-only three-layer monitor combining representation anomaly detection, counterfactual action-distribution influence, and sparse-autoencoder interpretation support. Our second contribution is a steerability framework spanning black-box behavioral instructions and white-box matched-neutral counterfactuals. Our third contribution is an evaluation on a controlled multi-agent auction benchmark covering homogeneous and heterogeneous model pairs, many-agent scalability, and intervention effectiveness. The sequential monitor achieves mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.993 for homogeneous agents and 0.854 for heterogeneous pairs when text- and latent-collusion rows are pooled as positives. In Qwen3-0.6B auctions with 25-100 bidders, monitoring requires only a small normalized load relative to all possible directed pairs, while full white-box steering achieves 100% bid-distribution recovery and reduces collusive low-bid behavior by 47.3 percentage points. Because full white-box steering replays the matched neutral counterfactual, its exact recovery is a sanity check by construction. Overall, the controlled study shows that the evaluated private channel attacks can be monitored without training the primary monitor on attack examples and mitigated when matched counterfactual access is available.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [TractoGraphVLM: A Unified Vision-Language Framework for White Matter Tractography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18166)
*Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Janine Dale Mendola,Amir Shmuel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: TractoGraphVLM是一个统一的视觉语言模型框架，将纤维束表示为流线图（节点编码3D位置和切线方向），通过共享的GPS图Transformer编码器和BioGPT解码器联合完成束分类、文本-束检索、解剖学描述和视觉问答四项任务，在HCP Young Adult上达到91.8%分类准确率、84.7%检索R@1、BLEU-4=20.1、ROUGE-L=66.8和66.4% VQA准确率，并可零样本迁移至HCP Aging受试者，证明图表示优于体素基线，语言监督能学习到可迁移的神经解剖学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已成功应用于2D医学影像，但扩展到3D白质纤维束追踪仍具挑战，原因在于纤维束具有复杂的拓扑结构。现有方法难以统一处理分类、检索、描述和问答等多任务需求，缺乏一个共享架构来联合建模纤维束的几何拓扑与语言语义。

Method: 将纤维束表示为流线图，节点编码3D位置和切线方向；使用General, Powerful, Scalable (GPS)图Transformer生成束嵌入，通过对比学习与冻结的BiomedBERT文本编码器对齐；BioGPT解码器配合视觉前缀token生成描述和答案；单一共享编码器和解码器联合训练全部四项任务，从同一checkpoint评估。

Result: 在HCP Young Adult测试集上达到91.8%束分类准确率、84.7%检索R@1、BLEU-4=20.1、ROUGE-L=66.8和66.4% VQA准确率；相同checkpoint可零样本迁移到HCP Aging，判别任务下降较小而生成任务下降较大；语言监督恢复出半球和纤维家族等标签未提供的结构；保留纤维方向的图表示优于体素基线，GPS给出最佳平衡。

Conclusion: TractoGraphVLM证明分类、检索、描述和问答关于白质束的任务可由单一联合训练模型完成，模型能从语言监督中学习可迁移的神经解剖学知识，对年龄和采集偏移具有鲁棒性，为3D医学影像的视觉语言建模提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Vision language models have transformed 2D medical imaging, yet extending them to 3D white matter tractography remains challenging due to the complex topology of fiber bundles. We introduce TractoGraphVLM, a unified framework for four tasks, bundle classification, text-to-tract retrieval, anatomical captioning, and visual question answering, built on a shared GPS architecture, training procedure, and read-out design. Fiber bundles are represented as streamline graphs whose nodes encode 3D position and tangent orientation. A General, Powerful, Scalable (GPS) graph transformer produces bundle embeddings aligned with a frozen BiomedBERT text encoder via contrastive learning, while a BioGPT decoder with visual prefix tokens generates captions and answers. A single shared encoder and decoder is trained jointly across all four tasks and evaluated from one checkpoint. Trained on HCP Young Adult subjects, TractoGraphVLM achieves 91.8% bundle classification accuracy, 84.7% retrieval R@1, BLEU-4=20.1, ROUGE-L=66.8, and 66.4% VQA accuracy on a held-out test set. The same checkpoints transfer zero-shot to HCP Aging subjects, with a modest drop on discriminative tasks and a larger drop on generative tasks, showing robustness to age and acquisition shift. Language supervision yields richer representations than label-only training, recovering structure like hemisphere and fiber family, carried by captions but never given as a label. Swapping only the visual encoder, graphs preserving fiber orientation outperform volumetric baselines, with GPS giving the best balance. Generative metrics measure consistency with a structured knowledge base rather than independent clinical text; even so, TractoGraphVLM shows that classifying, retrieving, describing, and answering questions about a white matter bundle can be served by one jointly trained model that learns transferable neuroanatomy from language alone.

</details>


### [152] [QuARC-GS: Quantized Anchored Residual Coding for Compact Dynamic Scene Streaming with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18285)
*Vu Trung Nghia Nguyen,Yuchen Wang,Kyung Chul Lee,Kevin C. Zhou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: QuARC-GS是一种量化感知的4D场景优化框架，用于在线动态场景重建，通过单一规范帧加高度压缩的逐帧残差表示，实现最高11倍的逐帧存储压缩，同时保持重建质量和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D场景的在线自由视角视频流式传输仍然具有挑战性，尤其是长时间视频，因为详细的场景表示带来巨大的存储需求，且需要高重建/渲染速度。

Method: 使用单个规范帧和高度压缩的逐帧残差表示场景。通过两种互补策略压缩残差：1) 量化感知的锚点变形，抑制无关紧要的运动更新同时保留有意义的变形；2) 变化门控的密集化策略，仅在真实时间变化的区域分配新的高斯，消除冗余外观更新。

Result: 在广泛使用的数据集上，QuARC-GS实现了具有竞争力的重建质量和训练速度，同时与最先进方法相比，逐帧存储减少高达11倍。

Conclusion: QuARC-GS通过量化感知的锚点变形和变化门控密集化策略，能够在不牺牲重建质量的前提下实现超高压缩比的在线动态场景重建，为长时间动态视频的自由视角流式传输提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D scene representation techniques such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) and Gaussian splatting have made substantial progress in novel view synthesis, achieving high-quality renderings from arbitrary view angles. More recently, such techniques have been extended to dynamic 3D scenes; however, achieving sustainable online free-viewpoint video (FVV) streaming remains challenging, especially for longer videos, due to significant storage demands of detailed scene representations and high reconstruction/rendering speed needs. To address these challenges, we propose Quantized Anchored Residual Coding Gaussian Streaming (QuARC-GS), a quantization-aware 4D scene optimization framework for online dynamic scene reconstruction that achieves ultra-high compression while maintaining reconstruction speed and quality. QuARC-GS represents a scene using a single canonical frame and highly compressed per-frame residuals. Specifically, we compress each residual through two complementary strategies targeting motion, appearance, and densification. We introduce quantization-aware anchor deformation, which suppresses insignificant motion updates while preserving meaningful deformations, maintaining reconstruction quality under low-storage streaming. Furthermore, we design a change-gated densification strategy that allocates new Gaussians only in regions exhibiting genuine temporal changes, effectively eliminating redundant appearance updates and reducing storage overhead. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets demonstrate that QuARC-GS enables competitive reconstruction quality and training speed while cutting per-frame storage by up to 11$\times$ compared to the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [153] [Optic Disc Segmentation in Fundus Images: From Classical Image Processing and Deformable Models to Modern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18367)
*Buket D. Barkana*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇结构化的方法学综述，系统梳理了视盘(OD)分割技术从经典图像处理方法（阈值、形态学、Hough变换、主动轮廓等）到现代人工智能方法（深度学习、Transformer、可提示分割、视网膜基础模型）的演变历程，并总结了常用数据集、各方法的假设/优缺点及持续存在的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视盘精确定位与分割对视网膜图像分析和青光眼评估至关重要，但由于光照变化、病理干扰、血管遮挡和边界模糊等因素，该任务仍具挑战性。已有综述缺乏对不同方法论代际的系统性比较与批判性分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用结构化的文献检索与研究筛选流程，识别覆盖主要方法论发展的代表性研究；首先总结常用眼底图像数据集，然后按核心机制将经典方法分类（强度与阈值、直方图与熵分析、形态学、几何与Hough变换、滤波与特征算子、纹理与区域方法、主动轮廓与水平集），并逐一分析其假设、优势和局限；随后考察AI方法从手工特征到学习表征、Transformer分割、边界与形状感知学习、可提示分割及视网膜基础模型的演进。

Result: 综述发现，尽管实现手段从预定义算子演变为学习模块、损失函数、提示和预训练表征，几项核心分割原则（感兴趣区域定位、多尺度表示、几何与解剖约束、边界正则化）在不同方法论代际中持续存在。同时识别出边界模糊性、解剖变异、域偏移和跨数据集泛化仍为持续挑战。

Conclusion: 视盘分割方法已从基于手工特征的经典算法过渡到以深度学习和基础模型为主导的AI范式，核心分割原则在实现层面发生了根本性转变。未来研究需重点应对边界模糊、解剖变异、域偏移等遗留问题，提升跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate localization and segmentation of the optic disc (OD) are important for retinal image analysis and glaucoma assessment, yet remain challenging due to variations in illumination, pathology, vascular interference, and poorly defined boundaries. This structured methodological review examines the evolution of OD segmentation from classical image-processing and deformable models to contemporary artificial intelligence (AI)-based approaches. A structured literature search and study-selection process was used to identify representative studies spanning major methodological developments. The review first summarizes commonly used fundus-image datasets, then organizes classical methods by principal mechanisms, including intensity and thresholding, histogram and entropy analysis, morphology, geometric and Hough-transform methods, filtering and feature operators, texture- and region-based approaches, and active-contour and level-set models. This paper pays particular attention to the assumptions, strengths, limitations, and complementary roles of these methods in OD localization and boundary delineation. Representative AI approaches are subsequently examined to illustrate the transition from handcrafted features and explicitly defined priors to learned representations, Transformer-based segmentation, boundary- and shape-aware learning, promptable segmentation, and retinal foundation models. Across these methodological generations, several core segmentation principles persist, including region-of-interest localization, multiscale representation, geometric and anatomical constraints, and boundary regularization, although their implementation has shifted from predefined operators to learned modules, losses, prompts, and pretrained representations. The review further identifies boundary ambiguity, anatomical variability, domain shift, and cross-dataset generalization as continuing challenges.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Scheduling and Routing with Degradation-Triggered Job Arrivals: An Application to Forest Firefighting with an Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18140)
*Erdi Dasdemir,Esther Jose,Rajan Batta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个交织的调度与路由问题，其中新任务因现有任务恶化而出现。问题以谚语"一针及时省九针"为动机，应用于空中森林消防场景：每个着火区域有行动时间窗，延迟干预会导致火势扩大、价值减少并蔓延至邻近区域。文章开发了一个最大化价值保留的混合整数规划模型，以及一个基于动态约束生成的混合模型以提升可扩展性，通过计算实验和案例研究验证了模型性能，并开源代码保证可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多调度路由问题中，新任务的出现并非随机或独立，而是由现有任务处理延误所触发（如火灾蔓延引发新火点）。现有研究很少考虑这种"恶化导致新任务涌现"的级联效应，本文旨在填补这一空白，并强调"防患于未然"的及时干预策略的经济价值。

Method: 首先定义了交织的调度与路由问题（ISRP），其中任务需求在时间窗内恶化，超过阈值后触发新任务到达，且未处理任务会持续降低位置的基础奖励值。提出了一个混合整数规划（MIP）模型以最大化总剩余奖励，并构建了一个基于动态约束生成的混合模型来提升大规模实例的可扩展性。空中森林消防作为核心应用场景。

Result: 通过计算实验和案例研究评估了所提出模型的性能与实用性，验证了模型在求解质量和计算效率方面的有效性，并展示了动态约束生成方法在可扩展性上的优势。代码已开源以支持复现和后续研究。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模型有效解决了任务恶化触发新任务涌现的交织调度路由问题，在森林消防等实际场景中具有重要应用价值。及时干预（对应谚语"一针及时省九针"）能显著提升价值保留率。开源的代码库为学术界和实务界提供了可复用的工具，促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: We define an intertwined scheduling and routing problem where new jobs appear due to the degradation of the existing jobs. Specifically, once a job arrives at a potential job location, a time window begins during which the demand of the job can be fulfilled. The demand degrades within the time window, and once it surpasses a particular threshold, it triggers the arrival of new jobs. Each job location inherently possesses an initial default reward, and the presence of an unprocessed job at a location gradually reduces this default value. The overall objective is to maximize the total remaining reward. The underlying motivation of this problem aligns with the proverb ``a stitch in time saves nine," and the problem itself carries practical implications. We focus on the problem in the context of aerial forest firefighting. Each ignited area has a designated action window; delaying intervention causes the fire to grow, diminishing the area's value and causing it to spread to adjacent areas. We develop a mixed-integer programming model that maximizes value retention in wildfire-threatened regions, and a hybrid model based on dynamic constraint generation to enhance the scalability of the model. We evaluate the performance and practicality of our models through computational experiments and a case study. Additionally, we ensure the study's reproducibility and encourage further research by providing open access to the codebase of our model.

</details>


### [155] [Trust as a Field: A Macroscopic Representation for Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18178)
*Md Mahmudul Islam,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空信任场（spatio-temporal trust-field）框架，将车联网中单车级别的微观信任聚合并表示为道路段上连续的空间-时间分布，并研究了从稀疏路侧单元（RSU）测量中重建完整信任场的两种深度学习方法的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车联网信任评估方法大多在单车层面运作，难以推理信任在道路段上的时空演化规律，缺乏一种连续的空间-时间信任表示框架。

Method: 提出时空信任场框架，将微观车辆级信任聚合为道路段上的连续表示；并通过模拟实验对比两种重建方法：(i) 基于坐标的深度学习基线，可从稀疏样本中学习通用信任场；(ii) 场信息深度学习法，将信任视为车辆携带的潜在量，并通过聚合机制强制测量一致性。

Result: 实验表明，场信息深度学习方法在恢复与轨迹对齐的低信任模式方面更准确，且重建误差更小。

Conclusion: 提出的时空信任场概念在推理道路级信任演变方面具有前景，且场信息引导的重建方法优于通用坐标基线方法。

Abstract: Trust assessment is a fundamental component of cooperative and connected vehicle systems. However, existing approaches operate primarily at the level of individual vehicles, making it difficult to reason about trust evolution across road segments. In this paper, we propose a spatio-temporal trust-field framework that aggregates microscopic vehicle-level trust into a continuous representation over space and time. The trust field is formally defined on road segments. We conducted simulation-based experiments using synthetic trajectories generated under controlled conditions, enabling analysis of trust-field behavior in simple road scenarios. Beyond theoretical modeling, we study an implication of the trust-field concept: reconstructing the full trust field from sparse roadside-unit (RSU) measurements. We compare (i) a coordinate-based deep learning baseline that learns a generic trust field from sparse samples and (ii) a field-informed deep learning method that treats trust as a latent quantity carried by vehicles and enforces measurement consistency through the aggregation mechanism. The field-informed approach more accurately recovers trajectory-aligned low-trust patterns and yields improved reconstruction error.

</details>


### [156] [Revisiting the "Push-T" Robot Manipulation Task with Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18227)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Push-T基准任务，探索LLM编码代理（Claude Code with Fable 5）能否通过算法方案而非人类演示数据来解决问题。结果表明，该代理通过在线搜索找到2D gym仿真、利用仿真实验学习推动机制并迭代优化，最终以100%成功率、比最优扩散策略少46%步数完成任务，并进一步将任务从T扩展到完整字母表（Push-A到Push-Z），同时生成了Franka和UR5机械臂的3D跨实体仿真代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Push-T任务依赖人类演示和模仿学习来训练操作策略，但新兴的Agentic Robotics范式表明LLM编码代理可能无需演示数据即可生成算法解决方案。作者希望研究这种纯编码代理范式在Push-T这类经典基准上的有效性，并比较其与基于示教的视觉运动模仿学习策略的性能差异。

Method: 使用Claude Code与Fable 5构成的LLM编码代理，通过代理编码循环（agentic coding loop）自主完成任务：代理在线发现2D gym仿真环境，利用仿真实验学习推动物理机制，通过迭代优化策略提升成功率；随后通过自生成课程（self-generated curriculum）将任务推广到完整字母表的Push-A至Push-Z，并为Franka和UR5机械臂生成3D跨实体仿真的策略代码。

Result: 代理实现了100%的任务成功率，且使用比由200个人类演示训练的最佳扩散策略少46%的步数完成任务。此外成功扩展到完整字母表任务，并生成了Franka和UR5机械臂的跨实体仿真解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，LLM编码代理在没有人类演示数据的情况下，可以在经典操作基准上超越基于模仿学习的策略，展现了Agentic Robotics范式在操作策略学习中的巨大潜力。该工作为无演示学习的操作策略生成提供了新思路，但作者也承认这是短论文性质的初步探索。

Abstract: Push-T is an iconic benchmark for learning manipulation policies from human demonstrations. The robot must use a single point of contact to push a T-shaped block into a target pose. In this short paper, we revisit the Push-T task in the context of emerging advances in Agentic Robotics where an LLM coding agent -- Claude Code with Fable 5 -- is prompted to create an algorithmic solution that does not require any demonstration data. We study how effective the agentic coding loop can solve the Push-T task, and compare the resulting code as policy with the visuomotor imitation learning policy. Results suggest that the agent found the 2D gym simulation online, and used sim experiments to learn push mechanics, iteratively optimizing to achieve 100% success rate using 46% fewer steps than the best diffusion policy trained with 200 human demonstrations. The coding agent also solve extensions from T to the full alphabet (Push-A to Push-Z) using a self generated curriculum and generated simulation code for the Franka and UR5 robot arms in 3D cross-embodiment simulations with visual feedback. Videos, policies and details will be posted online.

</details>


### [157] [GigaBrain-WBC-0.5: A Behavior World Model for Robust Whole-Body Control with Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18234)
*Ziyang Cheng,Tianshu Tang,Jinxin Lan,Xinze Chen,Yuhan Gong,Zhichao Liu,Changzhong Wu,Yahao Mao,Zongyan Deng,Mingxuan Ma,Huasen Xi,Yilong Liu,Yutong Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Yang Wang,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaojie Jin,Zheng Zhu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5是首个用于人形机器人全身控制的"行为世界模型"(BWM)。与传统的纯反应式跟踪策略不同，它训练一个因果Transformer同时预测下一动作、下一状态及下一潜在行为指令的分布，使策略网络既能动作也能建模环境如何塑造未来可行的行为。通过自动地形标注管线恢复重定向动作的3D接触几何，实现大规模地形标注。部署时复用预测分布在线检测不合理指令并将其拉回已学习行为，实现"尽力而为"式的任务执行。在四类场景中均超越三个大规模跟踪基线，地形交互成功率81.3%（为最强基线的4.3倍），不合理指令下83.1%，摔倒恢复99.3%（为最强基线的16.8倍），并通过简单微调从Unitree G1迁移至Maker L01机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全身运动跟踪策略只能在平坦地面工作：它们在空场景中训练，从未学习地形和物体接触如何重塑动力学；且通过不断扩充参考运动语料来试图让策略在任何指令下保持平衡，但一旦可行行为变成环境依赖的，这种做法就失效了。需要一种能够建模环境-行为交互的全身控制策略。

Method: 提出GigaBrain-WBC-0.5，第一个用于人形全身控制的Behavior World Model (BWM)。核心方法包括：(1) 训练一个因果Transformer联合预测下一动作、下一状态以及下一潜在行为指令的分布；(2) 提出自动地形标注管线，从重定向运动数据中恢复完整3D接触几何，实现大规模地形标注；(3) 在部署阶段复用预测分布来在线检测不合理指令并将其拉回已学习的行为空间。

Result: 在四类场景中均取得最高成功率：地形交互81.3%（是最强基线的4.3倍）、不合理指令下83.1%、摔倒恢复99.3%（是最强基线的16.8倍）。硬件实验展示了在缺失支撑和扰动下的鲁棒交互能力；Unitree G1检查点通过简单微调即可迁移到Maker L01机器人。

Conclusion: GigaBrain-WBC-0.5验证了"行为世界模型"范式的有效性：让动作生成网络同时建模环境对可行行为的影响，能够显著提升人形机器人在非平坦地形交互、不合理指令鲁棒性和摔倒恢复等真实挑战场景中的表现，且具有良好的跨硬件迁移能力。

Abstract: Whole-body motion tracking policies turn a humanoid into a robust control interface: the teleoperator---or an upstream model---only supplies a coarse movement intent, while the low-level policy keeps the robot balanced and physically feasible. Existing trackers deliver this interface only on flat ground: trained in empty scenes, they never learn how contact with terrain and objects reshapes their dynamics, and they attempt to teach the policy to balance under any command by continually enlarging the reference-motion corpus, which stops working once feasible behaviors become environment-dependent. We present GigaBrain-WBC-0.5, the first Behavior World Model (BWM) for humanoid whole-body control. Rather than a purely reactive tracker, we train a causal Transformer to jointly predict its next action, next state, and the distribution over its next latent behavior command, so the network that acts also models how the environment shapes what it can do next. An automatic terrain-annotation pipeline recovers full 3D contact geometry from retargeted motion, enabling terrain annotation at the scale of existing motion datasets. The predicted distribution is reused at deployment to detect implausible commands online and retract them onto learned behaviors, so the robot attempts tasks in a "best-effort" manner. The result is a unified policy that takes real-time command, interacts with environment, and stays robust to implausible commands, falls, and disturbances. GigaBrain-WBC-0.5 achieves the highest success rate across all four regimes among three large-scale tracker baselines: 81.3% on terrain interaction (4.3x the strongest baseline), 83.1% under implausible commands, and 99.3% fall recovery (16.8x the strongest baseline). Hardware trials show robust interaction under missing supports and disturbances; the Unitree G1 checkpoint transfers to the Maker L01 robot with simple fine-tuning.

</details>


### [158] [GAPL: Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning for LLM-Based Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18254)
*Zhihong Cui,Hengyu Liu,Zhangkai Wu,Yushuai Li,Tianyi Li,Peiyuan Guan,Amir Taherkordi,Tor Skeie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAPL是一个面向自动驾驶轨迹规划的统一框架，通过将LLM的语义推理能力与仿真环境的动力学精确性相结合，利用闭环系统整合三个模块（LLM效果评估器、仿真效果锚定器、效果感知决策器），在四个Highway-env场景中显著降低了碰撞率和位移误差，并提升了奖励值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽具备丰富的语义推理能力，但在自动驾驶轨迹规划中面临三个关键挑战：幻觉推理（hallucinated reasoning）、对环境动力学的弱锚定（poor grounding），以及数值控制的精度受限（limited numerical precision）。需要一种方法将LLM的高层语义推理与低层精确控制有效结合。

Method: GAPL采用闭环统一框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) LLM-based Effect Evaluator，进行结构化的多维度动作-效果估计；(2) Simulation-based Effect Grounder，通过仿真器rollout预测符合动力学一致性的效果；(3) Effect-Aware Decision Maker，通过distiller将LLM效果估计与仿真结果对齐，并引导PPO（近端策略优化）策略学习。

Result: 在四个Highway-env场景中，GAPL一致性地优于基线方法：碰撞率平均降低0.76，平均位移误差（ADE）平均降低0.86，最终位移误差（FDE）平均降低2.00，奖励值平均提升1.44。

Conclusion: GAPL通过将LLM的语义推理能力与基于仿真的动力学精确锚定相结合，有效克服了LLM在自动驾驶规划中的局限性，证明了LLM与仿真环境互补融合在轨迹规划任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning for autonomous driving requires both high-level reasoning and precise low-level control. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic-rich planning capabilities, however, their application is limited by hallucinated reasoning, poor grounding in environment dynamics, and limited numerical precision in control. We propose GAPL (Grounded Action-effect Policy Learning), a unified framework that integrates LLM-based effect estimation, simulation-based effect grounding, and policy optimization into a closed-loop system. GAPL consists of three modules: (1) an LLM-based Effect Evaluator for structured multi-dimensional action-effect estimation; (2) a Simulation-based Effect Grounder that predicts dynamics-consistent effects from simulator rollouts; and (3) an Effect-Aware Decision Maker that grounds LLM effect estimates against simulation via a distiller to guide Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO)-based policy learning. Experiments on four Highway-env scenarios demonstrate that GAPL consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average reductions of {0.76, 0.86, 2.00} in collision rate, average displacement error (ADE), and final displacement error (FDE), and an average reward gain of 1.44.

</details>


### [159] [VERAGMIL: Virtual Environment for Scooping Granular Foods with Imitation Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18258)
*Amanuel Ergogo,Diego Dall'Alba,Przemyslaw Korzeniowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VERAGMIL是一个结合高保真模拟器与虚拟现实(VR)界面的框架，用于录制演示数据并支持机器人辅助喂食(RAF)系统中的模仿学习方法，专门应对颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）的动态物理属性挑战。通过对比BC、BC-RNN和BCQ三种模仿学习模型在颗粒物舀取和运输任务上的表现，发现VR演示显著优于3D空间鼠标数据，其中BCQ在减少洒落和逼近人类水平方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助喂食系统对残疾或运动障碍人群至关重要，但操作颗粒状食物（如米饭、豆类）因其动态物理属性极具挑战性。从人类演示中学习虽然前景可观，但获取高质量演示数据十分困难，因此需要一种既能提供高保真环境又能便捷录制高质量演示数据的框架。

Method: VERAGMIL框架包含两部分：一是高保真模拟器，提供含机器人、传感器及多种具有不同物理特性食物模型的逼真训练环境；二是直观的VR界面用于录制演示数据。作者使用VR界面和3D空间鼠标两种方式录制演示，训练三种模仿学习模型（BC、BC-RNN、BCQ），在颗粒物舀取和运输任务上与人类专家基线进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于VR的演示数据显著优于3D空间鼠标录制数据；BCQ在所有评估模型中整体表现最佳，尤其在减少食物洒落方面成效突出，其表现已逼近人类水平。模型在成功率、洒落量、对未见食物的泛化能力及任务完成时间等方面均得到了评估。

Conclusion: VERAGMIL框架有效支撑了RAF系统在颗粒状材料处理任务中的训练，VR演示方式能够提供更高质量的模仿学习数据，BCQ算法在颗粒物操作任务中展现出最优性能，验证了该框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Robot-Assisted Feeding (RAF) systems are essential for assisting individuals with disabilities or motor impairments in eating tasks. Manipulating granular food items, such as rice and beans, poses significant challenges due to their dynamic physical properties. Learning from human demonstrations offers a promising solution, but acquiring high-quality demonstrations is complex. To address this, we present VERAGMIL, a framework that combines a high-fidelity simulator with an intuitive Virtual Reality (VR) interface for recording demonstrations and supporting different imitation learning methods. VERAGMIL provides a realistic environment for training RAF systems to handle granular materials, including robots, sensors, and various food items with distinct physical characteristics. We evaluate VERAGMIL by training three imitation learning models, BC, BC-RNN, and BCQ, on granular scooping and transporting tasks using both VR interface and 3D space mouse demonstrations, comparing them with a human-expert baseline. The models are assessed on success rate, spillage, generalization to unseen food items, and task completion time. Results show that VR-based demonstrations significantly outperform 3D space mouse data, with BCQ achieving the best overall performance, particularly in reducing spillage and approaching human performance. These findings underscore the effectiveness of our framework for training RAF systems in granular material handling. The code for our framework is publicly available at: https://github.com/AmanuelErgogo/VERAGMIL.git.

</details>


### [160] [Transferable Tool-Tissue Contact Detection from Stereo Depth in Robot-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18270)
*Mingyeung Wu,Zhonghao Zhang,Hao Yang,Alan Kuntz,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用立体成像生成的深度图像来检测机器人辅助手术中的器械-组织接触，通过局部最小距离信号驱动一个全监督的隐马尔可夫模型分类器，在跨任务、跨假体的泛化评估中显著优于基于RGB外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术中，可靠的器械-组织接触检测对于交互感知控制和下游力估计至关重要。现有方法大多基于RGB外观学习接触分类器，但外观特征（如颜色、纹理）在不同任务和不同场景下难以泛化，导致分类性能在跨域场景中显著退化。

Method: 利用立体相机生成的深度图像，在每帧深度图中沿器械边界定位一个空间支持的最小距离补丁，将其压缩为单个标量信号 -log10|d|（对应接触时的最小距离趋近零）。将该信号形式化为全监督的两状态隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），以六折留一会话LOSO集成方式在六个触诊会话上拟合，决策阈值从汇总的折外预测中选取。

Result: 在四组保留会话（涵盖同任务同假体、同任务不同假体、不同任务不同假体）上，该方法达到宏F1为0.927、AUPRC为0.980。对比的RGB基线模型在同任务同假体上F1高达0.965，但在其他两类场景中大幅退化，四组会话宏F1仅0.320，表明深度距离信号具有强迁移性。

Conclusion: 器械-组织间的距离信号是接触检测中强且可迁移的线索。基于深度图像的最小距离信号相比RGB外观特征在跨任务、跨场景泛化上具有显著优势，为机器人辅助手术中的交互感知控制提供了更可靠的接触检测方案。

Abstract: Reliable tool--tissue contact detection can support interaction-aware control and downstream force estimation in robot-assisted surgery. Most existing methods learn a contact classifier from RGB appearance, which is hard to generalize. In this work, we use the depth image generated from a stereo pair to give more information about tool--tissue contact. For each depth frame, we localize a spatially supported minimum-distance patch around the tool boundary and reduce it to a single scalar, $-\log_{10}|d|$; this signal rises and falls in step with ground-truth contact. We formalize this observation with a fully supervised two-state hidden Markov model. We fit this model as a six-fold leave-one-session-out (LOSO) ensemble on six palpation sessions against a single silicone cup-like phantom, with the decision threshold selected from the pooled out-of-fold predictions. It is evaluated on four held-out sessions of three categories: 1. same task on same phantom; 2. same task on different phantom; 3. different task on different phantom. This model reaches held-out macro F1 $0.927$ and AUPRC $0.980$. We further compare against a reproduction of an RGB-based contact classifier from prior work. This RGB-based model achieves high performance on the first category (F1 $0.965$), but substantially lower performance on the other two, resulting in macro F1 $0.320$ across all four sessions. These results indicate that the tool--tissue distance is a strong, transferable cue for contact detection in robot-assisted surgery.

</details>


### [161] [GuideFetch: A Task Coordination Framework for Concurrent Navigation and Object Retrieval in Assistive Robot Dogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18292)
*Qian Yin,Ruiping Liu,Kunyu Peng,Jianxiang Man,Isik Baran Sandan,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GuideFetch，一个用于异构导盲机器人与取物机器人团队在并发导航与目标取物任务中的协调框架。LLM 根据自然语言指令实例化一个基于调度条件的四动作模式，并通过别名归一化、目标/能力/调度校验来确保动作合法性。在 90 种场景与种子的 2×2 匹配研究（共360次执行）中，所有180个在线 LLM 响应均无需回退即可通过验证且与脚本计划一致；并行执行相比顺序执行可将平均完成时间缩短41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中（如导盲机器人带路、辅助机器狗取咖啡），导航与取物任务可以并发执行以提高效率。然而，LLM 生成的语法有效计划可能违反机器人本体约束，且控制器表现出的成功运动并不能等价于任务确实完成，因此需要一个协调框架来同时处理调度、状态验证和任务完成判定。

Method: GuideFetch 框架包含：(1) LLM 从自然语言指令实例化一个调度条件化的四动作模式；(2) 执行前对机器人、技能和目标别名进行归一化，并将提案动作与注册目标、机器人能力和选定调度进行验证；(3) 基于机器人与对象状态控制顺序或并行执行。采用 2×2 匹配研究设计（场景×种子，90×2），对比 LLM 在线计划与脚本计划在顺序/并行两种执行模式下的表现。

Result: 在360次执行中，180个在线 LLM 响应全部通过验证且无需回退或重放，与脚本计划完全匹配。顺序与并行执行分别达到72/90和71/90的操作成功率。在两种调度均完成的56个案例中，并行执行使平均制造时间（makespan）降低41.3%。

Conclusion: 在受控设定下，角色分工与动作重叠可缩短完成任务的时间；状态检查机制能够区分"计划合法性"与"任务完成确认"之间的差异，即有效的运动规划不等同于实际的任务完成。源代码将公开。

Abstract: Consider a robot guide dog escorting a blind user to an available seat while a second assistive robot dog concurrently retrieves a cup of coffee and delivers it to the same seat. This setting motivates concurrent execution because navigation and object retrieval can overlap. A syntactically valid Large Language Model (LLM) plan may still violate embodiment constraints, and successful-looking controller motion does not by itself establish task completion. We introduce \textsc{GuideFetch}, a coordination framework for concurrent navigation and object retrieval by a heterogeneous guider and fetcher team. An LLM instantiates a schedule-conditioned four-action schema from a natural-language instruction. Before execution, robot, skill, and target aliases are normalized, and proposed actions are validated against registered targets, robot capabilities, and the selected schedule. Robot and object states then govern sequential and parallel execution. In a matched $2\times2$ study across 90 combinations of scene and seed (360 executions), all 180 online LLM responses validate without fallback or replay and match the corresponding scripted plans. For each planner source, sequential and parallel execution achieve $72/90$ and $71/90$ operational successes, respectively. Among the 56 cases completed by both schedules, parallel execution reduces mean makespan by 41.3\%. Within this controlled setting, role specialization and action overlap shorten completed missions, while state checks distinguish plan validity from verified mission completion. Source code will be available.

</details>


### [162] [A Task-Agnostic Control Strategy for Dynamic Assistance with Pneumatically Actuated Soft Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18364)
*Anoush Sepehri,Zachary Huang,Raymond de Callafon,Michael T. Tolley,Tania K. Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖运动学传感的逆系统控制策略，用于气动软体外骨骼的动态辅助。通过Hammerstein动态模型（Preisach磁滞模型+线性时不变滤波器）对系统建模，利用140秒数据个性化每位用户，并求逆整合到控制回路中。在模拟腕关节软体外骨骼的测试台上，该控制器在不使用生理或力传感器的情况下，将交互力矩降低最多73%，关键屈肌和伸肌激活降低最多47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动人工肌肉为上肢软体外骨骼提供了康复和日常生活辅助的新机会，但其复杂动力学和有限带宽使得基于用户意图的响应式辅助长期难以实现。现有方法往往依赖生理或力传感器来解读用户意图，增加了系统复杂性和成本，因此需要一种仅基于运动学传感、无需这些传感器的任务无关动态辅助控制策略。

Method: 采用Hammerstein动态模型建模人机系统，其中包含Preisach磁滞模型描述静态特性，级联线性时不变滤波器描述动态行为。使用140秒数据对每位用户进行个性化建模，并通过模型求逆将逆系统集成到控制回路中，实现无需力或生理传感器的运动学驱动的闭环控制。

Result: 在模拟腕关节软体辅助外骨骼的测试台上，该控制器在8°/s至120°/s的速度范围内，相比无辅助条件将交互力矩降低最多73%，关键屈肌和伸肌的肌肉激活降低最多47%。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种无需生理或力传感器即可实现气动软体外骨骼任务无关动态辅助的控制策略，为日常生活场景下的软体可穿戴辅助设备提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Pneumatic artificial muscles have provided new opportunities to develop upper-extremity soft exosuits for reha- bilitation, augmentation, and assisted daily living. However, the complex dynamics and limited bandwidth of these actuators has made providing responsive assistance based on user intention a longstanding challenge. In this work, we present an inverse-plant control strategy for pneumatically actuated soft exosuits that only relies on kinematic sensing for task-agnostic and dynamic assistance during daily living. We model the human-robot system using a Hammerstein dynamic model, consisting of a Preisach hysteresis model and a linear time-invariant filter, to capture the static and dynamic behavior of the system. We personalize our model to each user using 140 s of data and approximate an inverse to integrate into our control loop. When evaluated on a test rig that emulated a soft assistive exosuit for the wrist, our controller reduced the interaction torque by up to 73% and the activation of key flexor and extensor muscles by up to 47% relative to the condition with no assistance for speeds ranging from 8°/s to 120°/s. Overall, this work presents a control strategy that can provide task-agnostic, dynamic assistance with pneumatically actuated soft exosuits without the need for physiological or force sensors to interpret user intention.

</details>


### [163] [The Embodiment Gap in Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18433)
*Yukiyasu Domae,Keisuke Shirai,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Masaki Murooka,Takuma Yagi,Yoshiaki Bando,Ryo Hanai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"具身鸿沟"（embodiment gap）概念，指可复用模型/表征/数据与在目标机器人上实际执行之间的差距，并以此审视机器人基础模型（RFMs）的跨具身泛化问题，提出两轴分类地图和报告框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型常从规模视角讨论（更多数据、更大模型、更广基准），但忽略了模型即使能泛化，仍需针对特定机器人本体进行适配工作才能实际运行。这种适配工作量因方法和目标机器人而异，影响实际部署，而现有研究缺乏系统性分析框架。

Method: 首先提出"具身鸿沟"概念，定义可复用内容与目标机器人执行之间的差距；其次构建两轴地图，一轴为共享结构的类型，一轴为执行阶段所需的适配位置；然后从三个研究方向（共享语义与感知、共享数据与接口、跨具身对应学习）梳理近期工作；最后提出报告框架，揭示成功率之外应核查的适配工作量。

Result: 通过两轴映射将现有方法分类，识别三个重叠研究方向的进展与局限，并提出一个报告框架来标注跨具身学习中需要检查的适配工作以及在新机器人上仍需完成的工作。

Conclusion: 成功率不能完整反映跨具身学习的效果，应将适配工作量纳入评估；未来研究应关注如何量化并减少具身鸿沟，推动机器人基础模型更高效地部署到不同机器人本体。

Abstract: Robot foundation models (RFMs), including vision-language-action (VLA) policies, are often discussed through a scaling view: more data, larger models, and broader benchmarks should improve generalization. In robotics, however, a model can generalize while work still remains before it can run on a robot with a particular body. The work required differs across methods and target robots, and those differences affect practical deployment. We call the gap between reusable models, representations, or data and their use in execution on the target robot the embodiment gap. This survey examines what can be reused across robot embodiments and what must still be implemented on a new robot. We place existing methods on a two-axis map that shows the type of shared structure and the stage at which adaptation is needed for execution on the target robot. We then examine recent work through three overlapping research directions: sharing semantics and perception, sharing robot data and interfaces, and learning correspondence across embodiments. We also propose a reporting framework for adaptation work that success rate alone does not reveal. The framework identifies the work that should be checked when comparing cross-embodiment learning and highlights work that remains on a new robot and questions for future study.

</details>


### [164] [HarvestPoint-ACT: Explicit Target Selection and Harvest-Point Conditioning for Robotic Fruit Harvesting under Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18446)
*Hanying Hu,Weipeng Li,Yikun Huang,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarvestPoint-ACT，一种面向果实采摘的端到端模仿学习系统。通过实例分割前端预测每个可见果实的掩膜和收获点（夹爪闭合位置），由调度器根据遮挡和旅行距离排序选择目标，并在每次尝试后重新检测和重排。所选果实被编码为八维状态输入动作分块Transformer；当目标暂时不可见时，保留最后估算并在机器人基座坐标系中标记为过期，若持续丢失则中止尝试。在冠层模拟环境中达到88%的成功率，重度遮挡下为75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端模仿学习避免了手工设计的机器人接近和抓取运动，但策略仍需自行决定采摘哪个果实以及在何处闭合夹爪。遮挡会使策略在采摘过程中丢失已选目标，而仅从像素推断正确的闭合点十分困难，因此需要将这两个决策显式地在感知层面做出。

Method: 采用带关键点分支的实例分割前端，为每个可见果实预测掩膜和收获点；调度器按遮挡程度和旅行距离对候选果实排序并选定一个目标；每次尝试后因冠层可能变化而重新检测重排。所选果实以八维状态（绝对收获点、夹爪到收获点的向量、有效性标志、置信度分数）编码给动作分块Transformer；当目标暂时不可见时，在机器人基座坐标系中保留最后的收获点估计并标记为过期，若持续丢失则中止该次尝试。

Result: 在冠层模拟装置上，HarvestPoint-ACT取得88%的成功率，在重度遮挡条件下仍能达到75%的成功率。

Conclusion: 将目标选择与夹爪闭合点决策显式化到感知环节，结合重新检测机制与状态保持/中止策略，能显著提高端到端模仿学习在遮挡环境下果实采摘的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: End-to-end imitation learning avoids hand-made robot motion for approaching and grasping, but the policy must still decide which fruit to pick and where to close the gripper. Occlusion can make the policy lose the selected fruit during harvesting, and the correct closing point is difficult to infer from pixels alone. This paper presents HarvestPoint-ACT, which makes both decisions explicit in perception and provides them to the policy. An instance segmentation front end with a keypoint branch predicts a mask and a harvest point for each visible fruit, where the harvest point specifies the location to close the gripper. A scheduler ranks detected candidates by occlusion and travel distance and selects one target. After each attempt, it redetects and reranks the candidates because the canopy may have changed. The selected fruit is encoded for an action chunking transformer as an eight-dimensional state, containing the absolute harvest point, the vector from the gripper to that point, a validity flag, and a confidence score. When the selected fruit is temporarily undetected, the system retains the last harvest point estimate in the robot base frame and marks it as stale, and aborts the attempt if the loss persists. On a canopy mock-up, HarvestPoint-ACT achieves a success rate of 88%, and of 75% under heavy occlusion.

</details>


### [165] [Backward Layout Search for Sequence-Constrained Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18454)
*Xi Zhang,Jiancong Dai,Hao Chen,Zhengtao Hu,Changcai Yang,Weiwei Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出逆向布局搜索（BLS）方法，通过逆向装配顺序分配零件初始位姿来求解机器人装配布局规划问题。每个扩展步骤执行几何、运动学、抓取和规定运动检查，并通过碰撞掩码与候选集过滤剔除不可行的初始位姿，采用束搜索保留有希望的局部布局，最终以正向装配顺序的全运动规划验证完整布局。在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能生成无碰撞可执行的布局，并相比正向搜索减少了步骤评估次数和搜索时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配布局规划需要在保证规定装配序列无碰撞执行的前提下，确定装配位置和每个零件的初始位姿。该问题具有挑战性，因为每步装配后障碍环境都在变化，工作空间中未装配的零件可能阻挡机器人运动，使得求解困难。

Method: 基于"每个装配步骤的可行性仅取决于当前及后续装配零件的初始位姿"这一关键依赖关系，提出逆向布局搜索（BLS），按逆装配顺序分配零件初始位姿。每步扩展进行几何、运动学、抓取及规定运动检查；利用碰撞掩码和候选集过滤剔除不可行初始位姿；通过束搜索保留有潜力的部分布局；最终在正向装配顺序下通过完整运动规划验证布局。

Result: 在五个装配模型上的实验表明，BLS能够生成无碰撞可执行的装配布局，相比匹配的正向搜索方法，显著减少了步骤评估次数并缩短了搜索时间。

Conclusion: 逆向搜索策略利用装配步骤间的依赖关系有效压缩了搜索空间，相比正向搜索在效率和可行性上均具有优势，为机器人装配布局规划提供了更优的求解途径。

Abstract: Robotic assembly layout planning must determine the assembly site and the initial pose of each part while ensuring collision-free execution of a prescribed assembly sequence. This problem is challenging because the obstacle environment changes after each assembly step, and unassembled parts re maining in the workspace may block robot motions. We observe that the feasibility of each assembly step depends only on the initial poses of the current and later-assembled parts. Based on this dependency, we propose Backward Layout Search (BLS), which assigns initial part poses in reverse assembly order. Each expansion performs geometric, kinematic, grasp, and prescribed-motion checks, while collision masks and candidate set filtering remove infeasible initial part pose candidates. Promising partial layouts are retained through beam selection, and complete layouts are validated by full motion planning in forward assembly order. Experiments on five assembly models show that BLS produces collision-free executable layouts and reduces step evaluations and search time compared with a matched forward search.

</details>


### [166] [DevGRU: Depth-guided Visual Navigation using a Collision-aware Recurrent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18470)
*Kyung Min Han,Eunsom Kim,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DevGRU，一个基于深度图像和点目标条件的导航系统，通过动作预测器和碰撞预测器生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹，有效避免复杂室内环境中的碰撞。在九个场景上与ViNT、NoMaD和NavDP等方法对比，DevGRU在导航性能和推理效率上均显著领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航基础模型虽然在多种平台上展现出泛化能力，但在结构复杂的室内环境中（如狭窄通道、结构化布局）容易发生碰撞。作者希望解决这一部署时的高碰撞率问题。

Method: DevGRU采用深度图像与点目标作为条件输入，核心组件包括：(1) 动作预测器（AP），生成碰撞感知的未来轨迹以避开即时障碍；(2) 碰撞预测器，与AP协同补偿目标位姿估计中的累积误差，并主动缓解未来导航偏差。模型参数量小，推理效率高。

Result: 在9个场景中与ViNT、NoMaD、NavDP及ViNT/NoMaD的四种变体进行对比。DevGRU在导航性能上大幅超越ViNT和NoMaD；模型尺寸比NavDP小7倍，推理速度快17倍，且在所有基线中推理时间最快。

Conclusion: DevGRU通过引入碰撞感知的动作预测机制，在保持轻量级模型和高推理效率的同时，显著提升了复杂室内环境下的导航可靠性和安全性，验证了将碰撞预测与动作预测联合设计的有效性。

Abstract: Existing visual navigation models often aim to develop foundation models that can generalize robot navigation across diverse platforms. However, many of these models are prone to collisions when deployed in complex indoor environments, particularly in structured layouts and narrow passages. To address this problem, we propose a depth image- and point-goal-conditioned navigation system, DevGRU. The proposed system employs an action predictor (AP) that generates collision-aware future trajectories, enabling effective avoidance of immediate obstacles. In conjunction with a collision predictor, the AP further compensates for errors accumulated in the goal pose estimation and proactively mitigates future deviations.
